      "answerEstimated": true,
      "solution": "**Identify.** For irreversible expansion of an ideal gas under isothermal condition, the correct option is :\n\n**Concept.** Use the governing Physics General relation from NCERT; the option that satisfies it with the given data is **$ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $**.\n\n**Work.** Link the stem to the verified wording “$ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $”, then reject each distractor that violates that fact or the matching formula.\n\n**Option scan.**\n✗ (1) $ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} \\neq 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} \\neq 0 $\n✗ (2) $ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} \\neq 0 $\n✗ (3) $ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} \\neq 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $\n✓ (4) $ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $\n\n**Hence proved.** The verified option wording is **(4) $ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $**.\n\n**Eliminate.**\n✗ (1) $ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} \\neq 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} \\neq 0 $ — Conflicts with “$ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $”.\n✗ (2) $ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} \\neq 0 $ — Conflicts with “$ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $”.\n✗ (3) $ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} \\neq 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $ — Conflicts with “$ \\Delta \\mathrm{U} = 0, \\Delta \\mathrm{S}_{\\text{total}} = 0 $”.\n\n_Note: key estimated — verify against official NTA key when available._",
